     * @return Whether the variable is only assigned a value once for its
     *     lifetime.
     */
Method code:
421 |     boolean isAssignedOnceInLifetime() {
422 |       Reference ref = getOneAndOnlyAssignment();
423 |       if (ref == null) {
424 |         return false;
425 |       }
426 |       
427 |       // Make sure this assignment is not in a loop.
428 |       for (BasicBlock block = ref.getBasicBlock();
429 |            block != null; block = block.getParent()) {
430 |         if (block.isFunction) {
431 |           break;
432 |         } else if (block.isLoop) {
433 |           return false;
434 |         }
435 |       }
436 | 
437 |       return true;
438 |     }

===== M0021 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java

Method code:
75 |   @Override
76 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
77 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
78 |     Renamer renamer;
79 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
80 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used, the starting context can not
81 |       // be a function.
82 |       Preconditions.checkState(
83 |           !declarationRoot.isFunction() ||
84 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));
85 |       Preconditions.checkState(t.inGlobalScope());
86 |       renamer = rootRenamer;
87 |     } else {
88 |       renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
89 |     }
90 | 
91 |     if (!declarationRoot.isFunction()) {
92 |       // Add the block declarations
93 |       findDeclaredNames(declarationRoot, null, renamer);
94 |     }
95 |     nameStack.push(renamer);
96 |   }

===== M0022 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 491-515
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
491 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
492 |     switch (n.getType()) {
493 |       case Token.STRING:
494 |       case Token.NUMBER:
495 |       case Token.NULL:
496 |       case Token.TRUE:
497 |       case Token.FALSE:
498 |         return true;
499 |       case Token.CAST:
500 |       case Token.NOT:
501 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
502 |       case Token.VOID:
503 |       case Token.NEG:
504 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
505 |       case Token.NAME:
506 |         String name = n.getString();
507 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
508 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
509 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
510 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
511 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
512 |     }
513 | 
514 |     return false;
515 |   }

===== M0025 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.isImmutableAndWellDefinedVariable(Var,ReferenceCollection)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 681-731
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether the reference collection describes a variable that
     * is initialized to an immutable value, never modified, and defined before
     * every reference.
     */
Method code:
681 |     private boolean isImmutableAndWellDefinedVariable(Var v,
682 |         ReferenceCollection refInfo) {
683 |       List<Reference> refSet = refInfo.references;
684 |       int startingReadRef = 1;
685 |       Reference refDecl = refSet.get(0);
686 |       if (!isValidDeclaration(refDecl)) {
687 |         return false;
688 |       }
689 | 
690 |       boolean isNeverAssigned = refInfo.isNeverAssigned();
691 |       // For values that are never assigned, only the references need to be
692 |       // checked.
693 |       if (!isNeverAssigned) {
694 |         Reference refInit = refInfo.getInitializingReference();
695 |         if (!isValidInitialization(refInit)) {
696 |           return false;
697 |         }
698 | 
699 |         if (refDecl != refInit) {
700 |           Preconditions.checkState(refInit == refSet.get(1));
701 |           startingReadRef = 2;
702 |         }
703 | 
704 |         if (!refInfo.isWellDefined()) {
705 |           return false;
706 |         }
707 | 
708 |         Node value = refInit.getAssignedValue();
709 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(value);
710 | 
711 |         boolean isImmutableValueWorthInlining =
712 |             NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(value) &&
713 |             (!value.isString() ||
714 |                 isStringWorthInlining(v, refInfo.references));
715 |         boolean isInlinableThisAlias =
716 |             value.isThis() &&
717 |             !refInfo.isEscaped();
718 |         if (!isImmutableValueWorthInlining && !isInlinableThisAlias) {
719 |           return false;
720 |         }
721 |       }
722 | 
723 |       for (int i = startingReadRef; i < refSet.size(); i++) {
724 |         Reference ref = refSet.get(i);
725 |         if (!isValidReference(ref)) {
726 |           return false;
727 |         }
728 |       }
729 | 
730 |       return true;
731 |     }

===== M0026 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.inlineNonConstants(Var,ReferenceCollection,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 254-327
Documentation:

Method code:
254 |     private void inlineNonConstants(
255 |         Var v, ReferenceCollection referenceInfo,
256 |         boolean maybeModifiedArguments) {
257 |       int refCount = referenceInfo.references.size();
258 |       Reference declaration = referenceInfo.references.get(0);
259 |       Reference init = referenceInfo.getInitializingReference();
260 |       int firstRefAfterInit = (declaration == init) ? 2 : 3;
261 | 
262 |       if (refCount > 1 &&
263 |           isImmutableAndWellDefinedVariable(v, referenceInfo)) {
264 |         // if the variable is referenced more than once, we can only
265 |         // inline it if it's immutable and never defined before referenced.
266 |         Node value;
267 |         if (init != null) {
268 |           value = init.getAssignedValue();
269 |         } else {
270 |           // Create a new node for variable that is never initialized.
271 |           Node srcLocation = declaration.getNode();
272 |           value = NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(srcLocation);
273 |         }
274 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(value);
275 |         inlineWellDefinedVariable(v, value, referenceInfo.references);
276 |         staleVars.add(v);
277 |       } else if (refCount == firstRefAfterInit) {
278 |         // The variable likely only read once, try some more
279 |         // complex inlining heuristics.
280 |         Reference reference = referenceInfo.references.get(
281 |             firstRefAfterInit - 1);
282 |         if (canInline(declaration, init, reference)) {
283 |           inline(v, declaration, init, reference);
284 |           staleVars.add(v);
285 |         }
286 |       } else if (declaration != init && refCount == 2) {
287 |         if (isValidDeclaration(declaration) && isValidInitialization(init)) {
288 |           // The only reference is the initialization, remove the assignment and
289 |           // the variable declaration.
290 |           Node value = init.getAssignedValue();
291 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(value);
292 |           inlineWellDefinedVariable(v, value, referenceInfo.references);
293 |           staleVars.add(v);
294 |         }
295 |       }
296 | 
297 |       // If this variable was not inlined normally, check if we can
298 |       // inline an alias of it. (If the variable was inlined, then the
299 |       // reference data is out of sync. We're better off just waiting for
300 |       // the next pass.)
301 |       if (!maybeModifiedArguments &&
302 |           !staleVars.contains(v) &&
303 |           referenceInfo.isWellDefined() &&
304 |           referenceInfo.isAssignedOnceInLifetime()) {
305 |           // Inlining the variable based solely on well-defined and assigned
306 |           // once is *NOT* correct. We relax the correctness requirement if
307 |           // the variable is declared constant.
308 |         List<Reference> refs = referenceInfo.references;
309 |         for (int i = 1 /* start from a read */; i < refs.size(); i++) {
310 |           Node nameNode = refs.get(i).getNode();
311 |           if (aliasCandidates.containsKey(nameNode)) {
312 |             AliasCandidate candidate = aliasCandidates.get(nameNode);
313 |             if (!staleVars.contains(candidate.alias) &&
314 |                 !isVarInlineForbidden(candidate.alias)) {
315 |               Reference aliasInit;
316 |               aliasInit = candidate.refInfo.getInitializingReference();
317 |               Node value = aliasInit.getAssignedValue();
318 |               Preconditions.checkNotNull(value);
319 |               inlineWellDefinedVariable(candidate.alias,
320 |                   value,
321 |                   candidate.refInfo.references);
322 |               staleVars.add(candidate.alias);
323 |             }
324 |           }
325 |         }
326 |       }
327 |     }

===== M0033 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.getScope()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 632-646
Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
632 |   public Scope getScope() {
633 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
634 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
635 |       return scope;
636 |     }
637 | 
638 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
639 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
640 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
641 |       scopes.push(scope);
642 |     }
643 |     scopeRoots.clear();
644 |     // No need to call compiler.setScope; the top scopeRoot is now the top scope
645 |     return scope;
646 |   }

===== M0034 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.toSource(Node,Format,CompilerOptions,JSTypeRegistry,SourceMap,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 654-691
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a tree to JS code
   */
Method code:
654 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
655 |       CompilerOptions options, JSTypeRegistry registry,
656 |       SourceMap sourceMap,  boolean tagAsStrict) {
657 |     Preconditions.checkState(options.sourceMapDetailLevel != null);
658 | 
659 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
660 |     MappedCodePrinter mcp =
661 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
662 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
663 |             options.lineBreak,
664 |             options.preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile,
665 |             options.lineLengthThreshold,
666 |             createSourceMap,
667 |             options.sourceMapDetailLevel)
668 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(
669 |             options.lineLengthThreshold,
670 |             createSourceMap,
671 |             options.sourceMapDetailLevel);
672 |     CodeGenerator cg =
673 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
674 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(mcp, options, registry)
675 |         : new CodeGenerator(mcp, options);
676 | 
677 |     if (tagAsStrict) {
678 |       cg.tagAsStrict();
679 |     }
680 | 
681 |     cg.add(root);
682 |     mcp.endFile();
683 | 
684 |     String code = mcp.getCode();
685 | 
686 |     if (createSourceMap) {
687 |       mcp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
688 |     }
689 | 
690 |     return code;
691 |   }

===== M0048 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.findDeclaredNames(Node,Node,Renamer)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 212-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
212 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
213 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
214 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
215 |     if (parent == null
216 |         || !parent.isFunction()
217 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
218 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
219 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
220 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
221 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
222 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
223 |       }
224 | 
225 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
226 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
227 |       }
228 |     }
229 |   }

117 |     static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments) {
118 |       final String defaultResource
119 |           = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
120 | 
121 |       Locale locale = Locale.getDefault();
122 | 
123 |       // ResourceBundle does caching.
124 |       ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
125 | 
126 |       String formatString;
127 |       try {
128 |           formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
129 |       } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
130 |           throw new RuntimeException
131 |               ("no message resource found for message property " + messageId);
132 |       }
133 | 
134 |       /*
135 |        * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
136 |        * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
137 |        * single 's.
138 |        */
139 |       MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
140 |       return formatter.format(arguments);
141 |     }

   * Initializes the instance state needed for a compile job if the sources
   * are in modules.
   */
Method code:
396 |   public <T extends SourceFile> void initModules(
397 |       List<T> externs, List<JSModule> modules, CompilerOptions options) {
398 |     initOptions(options);
399 |

401 |     fillEmptyModules(modules);

===== M0068 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 588-628
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is a literal value. We define a literal value
   * as any node that evaluates to the same thing regardless of when or
   * where it is evaluated. So /xyz/ and [3, 5] are literals, but
   * the name a is not.
   *
   * Function literals do not meet this definition, because they
   * lexically capture variables. For example, if you have
   * <code>
   * function() { return a; }
   * </code>
   * If it is evaluated in a different scope, then it
   * captures a different variable. Even if the function did not read
   * any captured variables directly, it would still fail this definition,
   * because it affects the lifecycle of variables in the enclosing scope.
   *
   * However, a function literal with respect to a particular scope is
   * a literal.
   *
   * @param includeFunctions If true, all function expressions will be
   *     treated as literals.
   */
Method code:
588 |   static boolean isLiteralValue(Node n, boolean includeFunctions) {
589 |     switch (n.getType()) {
590 |       case Token.CAST:
591 |         return isLiteralValue(n.getFirstChild(), includeFunctions);
592 | 
593 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
594 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
595 |              child = child.getNext()) {
596 |           if ((!child.isEmpty()) && !isLiteralValue(child, includeFunctions)) {
597 |             return false;
598 |           }
599 |         }
600 |         return true;
601 | 
602 |       case Token.REGEXP:
603 |         // Return true only if all children are const.
604 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
605 |              child = child.getNext()) {
606 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child, includeFunctions)) {
607 |             return false;
608 |           }
609 |         }
610 |         return true;
611 | 
612 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
613 |         // Return true only if all values are const.
614 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
615 |              child = child.getNext()) {
616 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child.getFirstChild(), includeFunctions)) {
617 |             return false;
618 |           }
619 |         }
620 |         return true;
621 | 
622 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
623 |         return includeFunctions && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n);
624 | 
625 |       default:
626 |         return isImmutableValue(n);
627 |     }
628 |   }

Original range: 1190-1197
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1190 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1191 |     int precedence = precedenceWithDefault(type);
1192 |     if (precedence != -1) {
1193 |       return precedence;
1194 |     }
1195 |     throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1196 |         Token.name(type) + " (type " + type + ")");
1197 |   }

===== M0091 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1974-1976
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1974 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1975 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1976 |   }

/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @nocompile annotation out of the inputs.
   */
Method code:
1465 |   private void hoistNoCompileFiles() {
1466 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1467 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1468 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1469 |

===== M0105 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.ReferenceCollection.isWellDefined()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 310-333
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines if the variable for this reference collection is
     * "well-defined." A variable is well-defined if we can prove at
     * compile-time that it's assigned a value before it's used.
     *
     * Notice that if this function returns false, this doesn't imply that the
     * variable is used before it's assigned. It just means that we don't
     * have enough information to make a definitive judgment.
     */
Method code:
310 |     protected boolean isWellDefined() {
311 |       int size = references.size();
312 |       if (size == 0) {
313 |         return false;
314 |       }
315 | 
316 |       // If this is a declaration that does not instantiate the variable,
317 |       // it's not well-defined.
318 |       Reference init = getInitializingReference();
319 |       if (init == null) {
320 |         return false;
321 |       }
322 | 
323 |       Preconditions.checkState(references.get(0).isDeclaration());
324 |       BasicBlock initBlock = init.getBasicBlock();
325 |       for (int i = 1; i < size; i++) {
326 |         if (!initBlock.provablyExecutesBefore(
327 |                 references.get(i).getBasicBlock())) {
328 |           return false;
329 |         }
330 |       }
331 | 
332 |       return true;
333 |     }

===== M0129 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 624-641
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
624 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
625 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
626 |         next = c.getNext();
627 |         if (c.isVar()) {
628 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
629 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
630 |           }
631 | 
632 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
633 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
634 |             c.removeChild(name);
635 |             Node newVar = IR.var(name).srcref(n);
636 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
637 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
638 |           }
639 |         }
640 |       }
641 |     }

===== M0134 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 567-614
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
567 |     private void extractForInitializer(
568 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
569 | 
570 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
571 |         next = c.getNext();
572 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
573 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
574 |         switch (c.getType()) {
575 |           case Token.LABEL:
576 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
577 |             break;
578 |           case Token.FOR:
579 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
580 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
581 |               if (first.isVar()) {
582 |                 // Transform:
583 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
584 |                 // to:
585 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
586 |                 Node newStatement = first;
587 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
588 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
589 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
590 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
591 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
592 |               }
593 |             } else if (!c.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
594 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
595 |               Node empty = IR.empty();
596 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
597 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
598 | 
599 |               Node newStatement;
600 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
601 |               // but are handled differently.
602 |               if (init.isVar()) {
603 |                 newStatement = init;
604 |               } else {
605 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
606 |               }
607 | 
608 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
609 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
610 |             }
611 |             break;
612 |         }
613 |       }
614 |     }

===== M0139 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 544-577
Documentation:
/** Traverses a function. */
Method code:
544 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
545 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
546 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
547 | 
548 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
549 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
550 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
551 | 
552 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
553 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
554 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
555 |     }
556 | 
557 |     curNode = n;
558 |     pushScope(n);
559 | 
560 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
561 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
562 |       // scope.
563 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
564 |     }
565 | 
566 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
567 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
568 | 
569 |     // Args
570 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
571 | 
572 |     // Body
573 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null && body.isBlock(), body);
574 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
575 | 
576 |     popScope();
577 |   }

===== M0141 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.TemplateTypeMap.checkEquivalenceHelper(TemplateTypeMap,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/TemplateTypeMap.java
Original range: 196-243
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if this map and the specified map have equivalent template
   * types.
   */
Method code:
196 |   public boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(
197 |       TemplateTypeMap that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
198 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thisKeys = getTemplateKeys();
199 |     ImmutableList<TemplateType> thatKeys = that.getTemplateKeys();
200 | 
201 |     EquivalenceMatch[] thatMatches = new EquivalenceMatch[thatKeys.size()];
202 |     Arrays.fill(thatMatches, EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH);
203 | 
204 |     for (int i = 0; i < thisKeys.size(); i++) {
205 |       TemplateType thisKey = thisKeys.get(i);
206 |       JSType thisType = getResolvedTemplateType(thisKey);
207 |       EquivalenceMatch thisMatch = EquivalenceMatch.NO_KEY_MATCH;
208 | 
209 |       for (int j = 0; j < thatKeys.size(); j++) {
210 |         TemplateType thatKey = thatKeys.get(j);
211 |         JSType thatType = that.getResolvedTemplateType(thatKey);
212 | 
213 |         // Cross-compare every key-value pair in this TemplateTypeMap with
214 |         // those in that TemplateTypeMap. Update the Equivalence match for both
215 |         // key-value pairs involved.
216 |         if (thisKey == thatKey) {
217 |           EquivalenceMatch newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MISMATCH;
218 |           if (thisType.checkEquivalenceHelper(thatType, eqMethod)) {
219 |             newMatchType = EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH;
220 |           }
221 | 
222 |           if (thisMatch != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
223 |             thisMatch = newMatchType;
224 |           }
225 |           if (thatMatches[j] != EquivalenceMatch.VALUE_MATCH) {
226 |             thatMatches[j] = newMatchType;
227 |           }
228 |         }
229 |       }
230 | 
231 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thisMatch, eqMethod)) {
232 |         return false;
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     for (int i = 0; i < thatMatches.length; i++) {
237 |       if (failedEquivalenceCheck(thatMatches[i], eqMethod)) {
238 |         return false;
239 |       }
240 |     }
241 | 
242 |     return true;
243 |   }

===== M0144 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 298-321
Documentation:

Method code:
298 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
299 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
300 |       return;
301 |     }
302 | 
303 |     try {
304 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
305 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
306 | 
307 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
308 |       sourceName = "";
309 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
310 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
311 | 
312 |       for (Node root : roots) {
313 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
314 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
315 |       }
316 | 
317 |       popScope();
318 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
319 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
320 |     }
321 |   }

   * Gets the {@code prototype} property of this function type. This is
   * equivalent to {@code (ObjectType) getPropertyType("prototype")}.

366 |   public ObjectType getPrototype() {

===== M0168 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.nodeTypeMayHaveSideEffects(Node,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1091-1112
Documentation:

Method code:
1091 |   static boolean nodeTypeMayHaveSideEffects(Node n, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
1092 |     if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
1093 |       return true;
1094 |     }
1095 | 
1096 |     switch(n.getType()) {
1097 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1098 |       case Token.DEC:
1099 |       case Token.INC:
1100 |       case Token.THROW:
1101 |         return true;
1102 |       case Token.CALL:
1103 |         return NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler);
1104 |       case Token.NEW:
1105 |         return NodeUtil.constructorCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler);
1106 |       case Token.NAME:
1107 |         // A variable definition.
1108 |         return n.hasChildren();
1109 |       default:
1110 |         return false;
1111 |     }
1112 |   }

===== M0182 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 158-192
Documentation:

Method code:
158 |   @Override
159 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
160 |     switch (n.getType()) {
161 |       case Token.NAME:
162 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
163 |         if (newName != null) {
164 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
165 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
166 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the Javadoc?
167 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
168 |           }
169 |           n.setString(newName);
170 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
171 |         }
172 |         break;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
175 |         // Remove the function body scope
176 |         nameStack.pop();
177 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
178 |         nameStack.pop();
179 |         break;
180 | 
181 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
182 |         // Note: The parameters and function body variables live in the
183 |         // same scope, we introduce the scope when in the "shouldTraverse"
184 |         // visit of LP, but remove it when when we exit the function above.
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.CATCH:
188 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
189 |         nameStack.pop();
190 |         break;
191 |     }
192 |   }

===== M0186 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.doInlinesForScope(NodeTraversal,ReferenceMap)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 194-222
Documentation:
/**
     * For all variables in this scope, see if they are only used once.
     * If it looks safe to do so, inline them.
     */
Method code:
194 |     private void doInlinesForScope(NodeTraversal t, ReferenceMap referenceMap) {
195 | 
196 |       boolean maybeModifiedArguments =
197 |           maybeEscapedOrModifiedArguments(t.getScope(), referenceMap);
198 |       for (Iterator<Var> it = t.getScope().getVars(); it.hasNext();) {
199 |         Var v = it.next();
200 | 
201 |         ReferenceCollection referenceInfo = referenceMap.getReferences(v);
202 | 
203 |         // referenceInfo will be null if we're in constants-only mode
204 |         // and the variable is not a constant.
205 |         if (referenceInfo == null || isVarInlineForbidden(v)) {
206 |           // Never try to inline exported variables or variables that
207 |           // were not collected or variables that have already been inlined.
208 |           continue;
209 |         } else if (isInlineableDeclaredConstant(v, referenceInfo)) {
210 |           Reference init = referenceInfo.getInitializingReferenceForConstants();
211 |           Node value = init.getAssignedValue();
212 |           inlineDeclaredConstant(v, value, referenceInfo.references);
213 |           staleVars.add(v);
214 |         } else if (mode == Mode.CONSTANTS_ONLY) {
215 |           // If we're in constants-only mode, don't run more aggressive
216 |           // inlining heuristics. See InlineConstantsTest.
217 |           continue;
218 |         } else {
219 |           inlineNonConstants(v, referenceInfo, maybeModifiedArguments);
220 |         }
221 |       }
222 |     }

===== M0196 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 235-278
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0203 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(Node,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1000-1062
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if calls to this function have side effects.
   *
   * @param callNode The call node to inspected.
   * @param compiler A compiler object to provide program state changing
   *     context information. Can be null.
   */
Method code:
1000 |   static boolean functionCallHasSideEffects(
1001 |       Node callNode, @Nullable AbstractCompiler compiler) {
1002 |     if (!callNode.isCall()) {
1003 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
1004 |           "Expected CALL node, got " + Token.name(callNode.getType()));
1005 |     }
1006 | 
1007 |     if (callNode.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
1008 |       return false;
1009 |     }
1010 | 
1011 |     if (callNode.isOnlyModifiesArgumentsCall() &&
1012 |         allArgsUnescapedLocal(callNode)) {
1013 |       return false;
1014 |     }
1015 | 
1016 |     Node nameNode = callNode.getFirstChild();
1017 | 
1018 |     // Built-in functions with no side effects.
1019 |     if (nameNode.isName()) {
1020 |       String name = nameNode.getString();
1021 |       if (BUILTIN_FUNCTIONS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(name)) {
1022 |         return false;
1023 |       }
1024 |     } else if (nameNode.isGetProp()) {
1025 |       if (callNode.hasOneChild()
1026 |           && OBJECT_METHODS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(
1027 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1028 |         return false;
1029 |       }
1030 | 
1031 |       if (callNode.isOnlyModifiesThisCall()
1032 |           && evaluatesToLocalValue(nameNode.getFirstChild())) {
1033 |         return false;
1034 |       }
1035 | 
1036 |       // Math.floor has no side-effects.
1037 |       // TODO(nicksantos): This is a terrible terrible hack, until
1038 |       // I create a definitionProvider that understands namespacing.
1039 |       if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isName()) {
1040 |         if ("Math.floor".equals(nameNode.getQualifiedName())) {
1041 |           return false;
1042 |         }
1043 |       }
1044 | 
1045 |       if (compiler != null && !compiler.hasRegExpGlobalReferences()) {
1046 |         if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isRegExp()
1047 |             && REGEXP_METHODS.contains(nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1048 |           return false;
1049 |         } else if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isString()
1050 |             && STRING_REGEXP_METHODS.contains(
1051 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1052 |           Node param = nameNode.getNext();
1053 |           if (param != null &&
1054 |               (param.isString() || param.isRegExp())) {
1055 |             return false;
1056 |           }
1057 |         }
1058 |       }
1059 |     }
1060 | 
1061 |     return true;
1062 |   }

===== M0211 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 121-160
Documentation:

Method code:
121 |   @Override
122 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
123 |     new NodeTraversal(
124 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
125 |         .traverseRoots(externs, root);
126 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
127 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
128 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
129 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
130 |     }
131 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
132 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
133 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
134 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our current Scope logic
135 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
136 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
137 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
138 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
139 |     //   function f() {
140 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
141 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
142 |     //   }
143 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
144 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a separate scope for catch nodes.
145 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
146 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
147 |         .process(externs, root);
148 | 
149 |     FindExposeAnnotations findExposeAnnotations = new FindExposeAnnotations();
150 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, findExposeAnnotations);
151 |     if (!findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties.isEmpty()) {
152 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
153 |           new RewriteExposedProperties(
154 |               findExposeAnnotations.exposedProperties));
155 |     }
156 | 
157 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
158 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
159 |     }
160 |   }

===== M0217 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeIterators.FunctionlessLocalScope.next()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeIterators.java
Original range: 69-100
Documentation:

Method code:
69 |     @Override
70 |     public Node next() {
71 |       Node current = ancestors.pop();
72 |       if (current.getNext() == null) {
73 |         current = ancestors.peek();
74 | 
75 |         // If this is a function node, skip it.
76 |         if (current.isFunction()) {
77 |           return next();
78 |         }
79 |       } else {
80 |         current = current.getNext();
81 |         ancestors.push(current);
82 | 
83 |         // If this is a function node, skip it.
84 |         if (current.isFunction()) {
85 |           return next();
86 |         }
87 | 
88 |         while (current.hasChildren()) {
89 |           current = current.getFirstChild();
90 |           ancestors.push(current);
91 | 
92 |           // If this is a function node, skip it.
93 |           if (current.isFunction()) {
94 |             return next();
95 |           }
96 |         }
97 |       }
98 | 
99 |       return current;
100 |     }

===== M0233 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1237-1297
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1237 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1238 |     // unknown
1239 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1240 |       return true;
1241 |     }
1242 |     // all type
1243 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1244 |       return true;
1245 |     }
1246 |     // equality
1247 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1248 |       return true;
1249 |     }
1250 |     // unions
1251 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1252 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1253 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1254 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1255 |           return true;
1256 |         }
1257 |       }
1258 |       return false;
1259 |     }
1260 | 
1261 |     // TemplateTypeMaps. This check only returns false if the TemplateTypeMaps
1262 |     // are not equivalent.
1263 |     TemplateTypeMap thisTypeParams = thisType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1264 |     TemplateTypeMap thatTypeParams = thatType.getTemplateTypeMap();
1265 |     boolean templateMatch = true;
1266 |     if (isExemptFromTemplateTypeInvariance(thatType)) {
1267 |       // Array and Object are exempt from template type invariance; their
1268 |       // template types maps are considered a match only if the ObjectElementKey
1269 |       // values are subtypes/supertypes of one another.
1270 |       TemplateType key = thisType.registry.getObjectElementKey();
1271 |       JSType thisElement = thisTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1272 |       JSType thatElement = thatTypeParams.getTemplateType(key);
1273 | 
1274 |       templateMatch = thisElement.isSubtype(thatElement)
1275 |           || thatElement.isSubtype(thisElement);
1276 |     } else {
1277 |       templateMatch = thisTypeParams.checkEquivalenceHelper(
1278 |           thatTypeParams, EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT);
1279 |     }
1280 |     if (!templateMatch) {
1281 |       return false;
1282 |     }
1283 | 
1284 |     // Templatized types. The above check guarantees TemplateTypeMap
1285 |     // equivalence; check if the base type is a subtype.
1286 |     if (thisType.isTemplatizedType()) {
1287 |       return thisType.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType().isSubtype(
1288 |               thatType);
1289 |     }
1290 | 
1291 |     // proxy types
1292 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1293 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1294 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1295 |     }
1296 |     return false;
1297 |   }

/**
   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not
   * a function.

===== M0239 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 105-156
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST: {
127 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
128 | 
129 |           // Add the function parameters
130 |           for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
131 |             String name = c.getString();
132 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
133 |           }
134 | 
135 |           // Add the function body declarations
136 |           Node functionBody = n.getNext();
137 |           findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
138 | 
139 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
140 |         }
141 |         break;
142 | 
143 |       case Token.CATCH:
144 |         {
145 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
146 | 
147 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
148 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
149 | 
150 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
151 |         }
152 |         break;
153 |     }
154 | 
155 |     return true;
156 |   }

===== M0244 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.isInlineableDeclaredConstant(Var,ReferenceCollection)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 452-483
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether the given variable is declared as a constant
     * and may be inlined.
     */
Method code:
452 |     private boolean isInlineableDeclaredConstant(Var var,
453 |         ReferenceCollection refInfo) {
454 |       if (!identifyConstants.apply(var)) {
455 |         return false;
456 |       }
457 | 
458 |       if (!refInfo.isAssignedOnceInLifetime()) {
459 |         return false;
460 |       }
461 | 
462 |       Reference init = refInfo.getInitializingReferenceForConstants();
463 |       if (init == null) {
464 |         return false;
465 |       }
466 | 
467 |       Node value = init.getAssignedValue();
468 |       if (value == null) {
469 |         // This constant is either externally defined or initialized indirectly
470 |         // (e.g. in an function expression used to hide
471 |         // temporary variables), so the constant is ineligible for inlining.
472 |         return false;
473 |       }
474 | 
475 |       // Is the constant's value immutable?
476 |       if (!NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(value)) {
477 |         return false;
478 |       }
479 | 
480 |       // Determine if we should really inline a String or not.
481 |       return !value.isString() ||
482 |           isStringWorthInlining(var, refInfo.references);
483 |     }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 207-237
Documentation:
/**
   * @return true if this node marks the start of a new basic block
   */
Method code:
207 |   private static boolean isBlockBoundary(Node n, Node parent) {
208 |     if (parent != null) {
209 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
210 |         case Token.DO:
211 |         case Token.FOR:
212 |         case Token.TRY:
213 |         case Token.WHILE:
214 |         case Token.WITH:
215 |           // NOTE: TRY has up to 3 child blocks:
216 |           // TRY
217 |           //   BLOCK
218 |           //   BLOCK
219 |           //     CATCH
220 |           //   BLOCK
221 |           // Note that there is an explicit CATCH token but no explicit
222 |           // FINALLY token. For simplicity, we consider each BLOCK
223 |           // a separate basic BLOCK.
224 |           return true;
225 |         case Token.AND:
226 |         case Token.HOOK:
227 |         case Token.IF:
228 |         case Token.OR:
229 |           // The first child of a conditional is not a boundary,
230 |           // but all the rest of the children are.
231 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
232 | 
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     return n.isCase();
237 |   }

===== M0264 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silenceThreshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silenceThreshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silenceThreshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;

===== M0266 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 766-848
Documentation:

Method code:
766 |     @Override
767 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
768 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
769 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
770 |       if (name == null) {
771 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
772 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
773 |           errorReporter.error(
774 |             "unnamed function statement",
775 |             sourceName,
776 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
777 | 
778 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
779 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
780 |         }
781 |         name = new Name();
782 |         name.setIdentifier("");
783 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
784 |       }
785 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
786 |       Node newName = transform(name);
787 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
788 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
789 |         // declaration.
790 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
791 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
792 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
793 |         // same line as the function keyword.
794 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
795 |             functionNode.getLp();
796 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
797 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
798 |       }
799 | 
800 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
801 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
802 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
803 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
804 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
805 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
806 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
807 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
808 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
809 |       if (fnName != null) {
810 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
811 |       } else {
812 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
813 |       }
814 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
815 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
816 | 
817 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
818 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
819 |         Node paramNode = transformNodeWithInlineJsDoc(param);
820 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
821 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
822 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
823 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
824 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
825 |         } else {
826 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
827 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
828 |           // (an error has already been reported).
829 |           Preconditions.checkState(
830 |               config.isIdeMode
831 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
832 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
833 |         }
834 |       }
835 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
836 | 
837 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
838 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
839 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
840 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
841 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
842 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
843 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
844 |       }
845 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
846 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
847 |      return node;
848 |     }

===== M0268 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1424-1460
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1424 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1425 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1426 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1427 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1428 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1429 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1430 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1431 |           continue;
1432 |         }
1433 |       }
1434 | 
1435 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1436 | 
1437 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1438 |       if (n == null) {
1439 |         continue;
1440 |       }
1441 | 
1442 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1443 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1444 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1445 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1446 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1447 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1448 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1449 | 
1450 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1451 | 
1452 |         externs.add(input);
1453 |         staleInputs = true;
1454 |       }
1455 |     }
1456 | 
1457 |     if (staleInputs) {
1458 |       repartitionInputs();
1459 |     }
1460 |   }

===== M0274 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1193-1201
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1193 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1194 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1195 |       return -1;
1196 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1197 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1198 |     } else {
1199 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1200 |     }
1201 |   }

===== M0279 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 288-309
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
288 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
289 |     if (isAllType) {
290 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
291 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
292 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
293 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
294 |       } else {
295 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
296 |       }
297 |     } else {
298 |       int size = alternates.size();
299 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
300 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
301 |       } else if (size > 1) {
302 |         return null;
303 |       } else if (size == 1) {
304 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
305 |       } else {
306 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
307 |       }
308 |     }
309 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneNode()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1699-1713
Documentation:
/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node, specifically excluding its children.
   */
Method code:
1699 |   public Node cloneNode() {
1700 |     Node result;
1701 |     try {
1702 |       result = (Node) super.clone();
1703 |       // PropListItem lists are immutable and can be shared so there is no
1704 |       // need to clone them here.
1705 |       result.next = null;
1706 |       result.first = null;
1707 |       result.last = null;
1708 |       result.parent = null;
1709 |     } catch (CloneNotSupportedException e) {
1710 |       throw new RuntimeException(e.getMessage());
1711 |     }
1712 |     return result;
1713 |   }

===== M0304 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.VerifyConstants.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 299-347
Documentation:

Method code:
299 |     @Override
300 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
301 |       if (n.isName()) {
302 |         String name = n.getString();
303 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
304 |           return;
305 |         }
306 | 
307 |         boolean isConst = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
308 |         if (checkUserDeclarations) {
309 |           boolean expectedConst = false;
310 |           CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
311 |           if (NodeUtil.isConstantName(n)
312 |               || NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(convention, n, parent)) {
313 |             expectedConst = true;
314 |           } else {
315 |             expectedConst = false;
316 | 
317 |             JSDocInfo info = null;
318 |             Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
319 |             if (var != null) {
320 |               info = var.getJSDocInfo();
321 |             }
322 | 
323 |             if (info != null && info.isConstant()) {
324 |               expectedConst = true;
325 |             } else {
326 |               expectedConst = false;
327 |             }
328 |           }
329 | 
330 |           if (expectedConst) {
331 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
332 |                 "The name %s is not annotated as constant.", name);
333 |           } else {
334 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
335 |                 "The name %s should not be annotated as constant.", name);
336 |           }
337 |         }
338 | 
339 |         Boolean value = constantMap.get(name);
340 |         if (value == null) {
341 |           constantMap.put(name, isConst);
342 |         } else {
343 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.booleanValue() == isConst,
344 |               "The name %s is not consistently annotated as constant.", name);
345 |         }
346 |       }
347 |     }

===== M0306 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalVarReferenceMap.updateGlobalVarReferences(Map<Var, ReferenceCollection>,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalVarReferenceMap.java
Original range: 101-120
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the internal reference map based on the provided parameters. If
   * {@code scriptRoot} is not SCRIPT, it basically replaces the internal map
   * with the new one, otherwise it replaces all the information associated to
   * the given script.
   *
   * @param refMapPatch The reference map result of a
   *     {@link ReferenceCollectingCallback} pass which might be collected from
   *     the whole AST or just a sub-tree associated to a SCRIPT node.
   * @param root AST sub-tree root on which reference collection was done.
   */
Method code:
101 |   void updateGlobalVarReferences(Map<Var, ReferenceCollection>
102 |       refMapPatch, Node root) {
103 |     if (refMap == null || !root.isScript()) {
104 |       resetGlobalVarReferences(refMapPatch);
105 |       return;
106 |     }
107 | 
108 |     InputId inputId = root.getInputId();
109 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
110 |     // Note there are two assumptions here (i) the order of compiler inputs
111 |     // has not changed and (ii) all references are in the order they appear
112 |     // in AST (this is enforced in ReferenceCollectionCallback).
113 |     removeScriptReferences(inputId);
114 |     for (Entry<Var, ReferenceCollection> entry : refMapPatch.entrySet()) {
115 |       Var var = entry.getKey();
116 |       if (var.isGlobal()) {
117 |         replaceReferences(var.getName(), inputId, entry.getValue());
118 |       }
119 |     }
120 |   }

===== M0307 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 384-417
Documentation:

Method code:
384 |     @Override
385 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
386 |       switch (n.getType()) {
387 |         case Token.WHILE:
388 |           if (CONVERT_WHILE_TO_FOR) {
389 |             Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
390 |             n.setType(Token.FOR);
391 |             Node empty = IR.empty();
392 |             empty.copyInformationFrom(n);
393 |             n.addChildBefore(empty, expr);
394 |             n.addChildAfter(empty.cloneNode(), expr);
395 |             reportCodeChange("WHILE node");
396 |           }
397 |           break;
398 | 
399 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
400 |           normalizeFunctionDeclaration(n);
401 |           break;
402 | 
403 |         case Token.NAME:
404 |         case Token.STRING:
405 |         case Token.STRING_KEY:
406 |         case Token.GETTER_DEF:
407 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:
408 |           if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalizedObfuscated()) {
409 |             annotateConstantsByConvention(n, parent);
410 |           }
411 |           break;
412 | 
413 |         case Token.CAST:
414 |           parent.replaceChild(n, n.removeFirstChild());
415 |           break;
416 |       }
417 |     }

Documentation:

Method code:
791 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
792 |     boolean valid = false;
793 |     if (i == 1) {
794 |       valid = n.hasChildren();
795 |     } else if (i == 2) {
796 |       valid = n.hasMoreThanOneChild();
797 |     } else {
798 |       valid = n.getChildCount() >= i;
799 |     }
800 | 
801 |     if (!valid) {
802 |       violation(
803 |           "Expected at least " + i + " children, but was "
804 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
805 |     }
806 |   }

     */
Method code:
467 |     private Reference getOneAndOnlyAssignment() {
468 |       Reference assignment = null;
469 |       int size = references.size();
470 |       for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
471 |         Reference ref = references.get(i);
472 |         if (ref.isLvalue() || ref.isInitializingDeclaration()) {
473 |           if (assignment == null) {
474 |             assignment = ref;
475 |           } else {
476 |             return null;
477 |           }
478 |         }
479 |       }
480 |       return assignment;
481 |     }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.

===== M0337 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.canInline(Reference,Reference,Reference)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 517-581
Documentation:
/**
     * @return true if the provided reference and declaration can be safely
     *         inlined according to our criteria
     */
Method code:
517 |     private boolean canInline(
518 |         Reference declaration,
519 |         Reference initialization,
520 |         Reference reference) {
521 |       if (!isValidDeclaration(declaration)
522 |           || !isValidInitialization(initialization)
523 |           || !isValidReference(reference)) {
524 |         return false;
525 |       }
526 | 
527 |       // If the value is read more than once, skip it.
528 |       // VAR declarations and EXPR_RESULT don't need the value, but other
529 |       // ASSIGN expressions parents do.
530 |       if (declaration != initialization &&
531 |           !initialization.getGrandparent().isExprResult()) {
532 |         return false;
533 |       }
534 | 
535 |       // Be very conservative and do no cross control structures or
536 |       // scope boundaries
537 |       if (declaration.getBasicBlock() != initialization.getBasicBlock()
538 |           || declaration.getBasicBlock() != reference.getBasicBlock()) {
539 |         return false;
540 |       }
541 | 
542 |       // Do not inline into a call node. This would change
543 |       // the context in which it was being called. For example,
544 |       //   var a = b.c;
545 |       //   a();
546 |       // should not be inlined, because it calls a in the context of b
547 |       // rather than the context of the window.
548 |       //   var a = b.c;
549 |       //   f(a)
550 |       // is OK.
551 |       Node value = initialization.getAssignedValue();
552 |       Preconditions.checkState(value != null);
553 |       if (value.isGetProp()
554 |           && reference.getParent().isCall()
555 |           && reference.getParent().getFirstChild() == reference.getNode()) {
556 |         return false;
557 |       }
558 | 
559 |       if (value.isFunction()) {
560 |         Node callNode = reference.getParent();
561 |         if (reference.getParent().isCall()) {
562 |           CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
563 |           // Bug 2388531: Don't inline subclass definitions into class defining
564 |           // calls as this confused class removing logic.
565 |           SubclassRelationship relationship =
566 |               convention.getClassesDefinedByCall(callNode);
567 |           if (relationship != null) {
568 |             return false;
569 |           }
570 | 
571 |           // issue 668: Don't inline singleton getter methods
572 |           // calls as this confused class removing logic.
573 |           if (convention.getSingletonGetterClassName(callNode) != null) {
574 |             return false;
575 |           }
576 |         }
577 |       }
578 | 
579 |       return canMoveAggressively(value) ||
580 |           canMoveModerately(initialization, reference);
581 |     }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
529 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
530 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
531 |          current != null;
532 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
533 |       if (current.isTemplatizedType()) {
534 |         current = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType().getReferencedType();
535 |       }
536 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
537 |         return true;
538 |       }
539 |     }
540 |     return false;
541 |   }

===== M0356 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.unrollBinaryOperator(Node,int,String,Context,Context,int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 780-796
Documentation:
/**
   * We could use addList recursively here, but sometimes we produce
   * very deeply nested operators and run out of stack space, so we
   * just unroll the recursion when possible.
   *
   * We assume nodes are left-recursive.
   */
Method code:
780 |   private void unrollBinaryOperator(
781 |       Node n, int op, String opStr, Context context,
782 |       Context rhsContext, int leftPrecedence, int rightPrecedence) {
783 |     Node firstNonOperator = n.getFirstChild();
784 |     while (firstNonOperator.getType() == op) {
785 |       firstNonOperator = firstNonOperator.getFirstChild();
786 |     }
787 | 
788 |     addExpr(firstNonOperator, leftPrecedence, context);
789 | 
790 |     Node current = firstNonOperator;
791 |     do {
792 |       current = current.getParent();
793 |       cc.addOp(opStr, true);
794 |       addExpr(current.getFirstChild().getNext(), rightPrecedence, rhsContext);
795 |     } while (current != n);
796 |   }

===== M0357 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 157-179
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
157 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
158 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
159 | 
160 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
161 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
162 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
163 | 
164 |       // ignore cast nodes.
165 |       while (first.isCast()) {
166 |         first = first.getFirstChild();
167 |       }
168 | 
169 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {

174 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".

178 |       }

===== M0358 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1514-1565
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recur Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @param shallow If true, the method doesn't recur into inner functions.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1514 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(
1515 |       Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recur, boolean shallow) {
1516 |     if (type != node.getType()
1517 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1518 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1519 |       return false;
1520 |     }
1521 | 
1522 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1523 |       return false;
1524 |     }
1525 | 
1526 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1527 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1528 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1529 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1530 |         return false;
1531 |       }
1532 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1533 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1534 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1535 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1536 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1537 |           return false;
1538 |         }
1539 |       }
1540 | 
1541 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1542 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1543 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1544 |         return false;
1545 |       }
1546 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1547 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1548 |         return false;
1549 |       }
1550 |     }
1551 | 
1552 |     if (recur) {
1553 |       Node n, n2;
1554 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1555 |            n != null;
1556 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1557 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(
1558 |             n2, compareJsType, !(shallow && n.isFunction()), shallow)) {
1559 |           return false;
1560 |         }
1561 |       }
1562 |     }
1563 | 
1564 |     return true;
1565 |   }
