===== M0374 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 790-935
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
790 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
791 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
792 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
793 |     // that we know to be safe
794 |     switch (n.getType()) {
795 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
796 |       case Token.CAST:
797 |       case Token.AND:
798 |       case Token.BLOCK:
799 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
800 |       case Token.HOOK:
801 |       case Token.IF:
802 |       case Token.IN:
803 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
804 |       case Token.NUMBER:
805 |       case Token.OR:
806 |       case Token.THIS:
807 |       case Token.TRUE:
808 |       case Token.FALSE:
809 |       case Token.NULL:
810 |       case Token.STRING:
811 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
812 |       case Token.SWITCH:
813 |       case Token.TRY:
814 |       case Token.EMPTY:
815 |         break;
816 | 
817 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
818 |       case Token.THROW:
819 |         return true;
820 | 
821 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
822 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
823 |           return true;
824 |         }
825 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
826 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
827 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
828 |             return true;
829 |           }
830 |         }
831 |         return false;
832 | 
833 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
834 |       case Token.REGEXP:
835 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
836 |           return true;
837 |         }
838 |         break;
839 | 
840 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
841 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
842 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
843 |           return true;
844 |         }
845 |         break;
846 | 
847 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
848 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
849 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
850 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
851 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
852 | 
853 |       case Token.NEW:
854 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
855 |           return true;
856 |         }
857 | 
858 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
859 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
860 |           // side-effects
861 |           break;
862 |         }
863 |         return true;
864 | 
865 |       case Token.CALL:
866 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
867 |         // side effect property set.
868 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
869 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
870 |           // side-effects
871 |           break;
872 |         }
873 |         return true;
874 | 
875 |       default:
876 |         if (isSimpleOperator(n)) {
877 |           break;
878 |         }
879 | 
880 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
881 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
882 |           if (assignTarget.isName()) {
883 |             return true;
884 |           }
885 | 
886 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
887 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
888 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
889 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
890 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
891 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
892 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
893 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
894 |             return true;
895 |           }
896 | 
897 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
898 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
899 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
900 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
901 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
902 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
903 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
904 |               return false;
905 |             }
906 | 
907 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
908 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
909 |             // other objects.
910 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
911 |             // side-effect.
912 |             while (isGet(current)) {
913 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
914 |             }
915 | 
916 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
917 |           } else {
918 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
919 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
920 |             // preserve existing behavior.
921 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
922 |           }
923 |         }
924 | 
925 |         return true;
926 |     }
927 | 
928 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
929 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
930 |         return true;
931 |       }
932 |     }
933 | 
934 |     return false;
935 |   }

===== M0401 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 505-521
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
505 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
506 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
507 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
508 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
509 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
510 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
511 |         String inputName = input.getName();
512 | 
513 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
514 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
515 |         // will get an error down the line.
516 |         inputs.add(input);
517 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
518 |       }
519 |     }
520 |     return inputs;
521 |   }

179 |                                           node.getClass() +

182 |         }

196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);

220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);

257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);

===== M0416 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.removeChild(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1827-1880
Documentation:
/** Safely remove children while maintaining a valid node structure. */
Method code:
1827 |   static void removeChild(Node parent, Node node) {
1828 |     if (isTryFinallyNode(parent, node)) {
1829 |       if (NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(getCatchBlock(parent))) {
1830 |         // A finally can only be removed if there is a catch.
1831 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1832 |       } else {
1833 |         // Otherwise, only its children can be removed.
1834 |         node.detachChildren();
1835 |       }
1836 |     } else if (node.isCatch()) {
1837 |       // The CATCH can can only be removed if there is a finally clause.
1838 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent().getParent();
1839 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1840 |       node.detachFromParent();
1841 |     } else if (isTryCatchNodeContainer(node)) {
1842 |       // The container node itself can't be removed, but the contained CATCH
1843 |       // can if there is a 'finally' clause
1844 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent();
1845 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1846 |       node.detachChildren();
1847 |     } else if (node.isBlock()) {
1848 |       // Simply empty the block.  This maintains source location and
1849 |       // "synthetic"-ness.
1850 |       node.detachChildren();
1851 |     } else if (isStatementBlock(parent)
1852 |         || isSwitchCase(node)) {
1853 |       // A statement in a block can simply be removed.
1854 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1855 |     } else if (parent.isVar()) {
1856 |       if (parent.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1857 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1858 |       } else {
1859 |         // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1860 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1861 |         // This would leave an empty VAR, remove the VAR itself.
1862 |         removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1863 |       }
1864 |     } else if (parent.isLabel()
1865 |         && node == parent.getLastChild()) {
1866 |       // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1867 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1868 |       // A LABEL without children can not be referred to, remove it.
1869 |       removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1870 |     } else if (parent.isFor()
1871 |         && parent.getChildCount() == 4) {
1872 |       // Only Token.FOR can have an Token.EMPTY other control structure
1873 |       // need something for the condition. Others need to be replaced
1874 |       // or the structure removed.
1875 |       parent.replaceChild(node, IR.empty());
1876 |     } else {
1877 |       throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid attempt to remove node: " +
1878 |           node.toString() + " of " + parent.toString());
1879 |     }
1880 |   }

===== M0417 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 209-237
Documentation:

Method code:
209 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
210 |     maybeEndStatement();
211 | 
212 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
213 |     char prev = getLastChar();
214 | 
215 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
216 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
217 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
218 |       append(" ");
219 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
220 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
221 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
222 |       append(" ");
223 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
224 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
225 |       append(" ");
226 |     }
227 | 
228 |     // Allow formatting around the operator.
229 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
230 | 
231 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
232 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
233 |     // because it looks strange.
234 |     if (binOp) {
235 |       maybeCutLine();
236 |     }
237 |   }

===== M0424 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.reconcileOptionsWithGuards()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 313-352
Documentation:
/**
   * When the CompilerOptions and its WarningsGuard overlap, reconcile
   * any discrepencies.
   */
Method code:
313 |   protected void reconcileOptionsWithGuards() {
314 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
315 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
316 |       options.checkTypes = true;
317 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
318 |       options.checkTypes = false;
319 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
320 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
321 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
322 |       // parser type warnings.
323 |       options.setWarningLevel(
324 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
325 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
326 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
330 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
331 |       options.setWarningLevel(
332 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
333 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
337 |       options.setWarningLevel(
338 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
339 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
340 |     }
341 | 
342 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
343 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
344 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
345 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
346 |     // error off.
347 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
348 |         !options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
349 |       options.setWarningLevel(
350 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF);
351 |     }
352 |   }

===== M0430 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1295-1419
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1295 |   Node parseInputs() {
1296 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1297 | 
1298 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1299 |     // individual file parse trees.
1300 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1301 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1302 |     }
1303 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1304 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1305 |     }
1306 | 
1307 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1308 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1309 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1310 | 
1311 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1312 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1313 | 
1314 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1315 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1316 | 
1317 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1318 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1319 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1320 |     }
1321 | 
1322 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer(PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1323 | 
1324 |     try {
1325 |       // Parse externs sources.
1326 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1327 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1328 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1329 |           return null;
1330 |         }
1331 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1332 |       }
1333 | 
1334 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1335 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1336 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1337 |       }
1338 | 
1339 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1340 | 
1341 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1342 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1343 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1344 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1345 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1346 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1347 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1348 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1349 |           }
1350 |         }
1351 | 
1352 |         try {
1353 |           inputs =
1354 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1355 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1356 |           staleInputs = true;
1357 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1358 |           report(JSError.make(
1359 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1360 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1361 |           report(JSError.make(
1362 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1363 |         } catch (JSModuleGraph.MissingModuleException e) {
1364 |           report(JSError.make(
1365 |               MISSING_MODULE_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1366 |         }
1367 | 
1368 |         // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1369 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1370 |           return null;
1371 |         }
1372 |       }
1373 | 
1374 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1375 | 
1376 |       if (staleInputs) {
1377 |         repartitionInputs();
1378 |       }
1379 | 
1380 |       // Build the AST.
1381 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1382 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1383 |         if (n == null) {
1384 |           continue;
1385 |         }
1386 | 
1387 |         if (devMode) {
1388 |           runSanityCheck();
1389 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1390 |             return null;
1391 |           }
1392 |         }
1393 | 
1394 |         // TODO(johnlenz): we shouldn't need to check both isExternExportsEnabled and
1395 |         // externExportsPath.
1396 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1397 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null ||
1398 |             options.isExternExportsEnabled() ||
1399 |             options.externExportsPath != null) {
1400 | 
1401 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1402 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1403 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1404 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1405 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1406 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1407 |         }
1408 | 
1409 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1410 |       }
1411 | 
1412 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1413 |         return null;
1414 |       }
1415 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1416 |     } finally {
1417 |       stopTracer(tracer, PARSING_PASS_NAME);
1418 |     }
1419 |   }

===== M0452 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.endFile()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 514-542
Documentation:

Method code:
514 |     @Override
515 |     void endFile() {
516 |       super.endFile();
517 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
518 |         return;
519 |       }
520 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
521 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
522 |         append(";");
523 |         startNewLine();
524 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
525 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
526 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
527 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
528 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
529 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
530 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
531 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
532 |         lineIndex--;
533 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
534 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
535 |         append(";");
536 |         startNewLine();
537 |       } else {
538 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
539 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
540 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
541 |       }
542 |     }

===== M0462 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 70-87
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
70 |   @Override
71 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
72 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
73 |       return false;
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
77 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
78 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
79 |     if (pos >= 0) {
80 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
81 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
82 |         return false;
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 | 
86 |     return isConstantKey(name);
87 |   }

===== M0473 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.maybeEscapedOrModifiedArguments(Scope,ReferenceMap)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 224-244
Documentation:

Method code:
224 |     private boolean maybeEscapedOrModifiedArguments(
225 |         Scope scope, ReferenceMap referenceMap) {
226 |       if (scope.isLocal()) {
227 |         Var arguments = scope.getArgumentsVar();
228 |         ReferenceCollection refs = referenceMap.getReferences(arguments);
229 |         if (refs != null && !refs.references.isEmpty()) {
230 |           for (Reference ref : refs.references) {
231 |             Node refNode = ref.getNode();
232 |             Node refParent = ref.getParent();
233 |             // Any reference that is not a read of the arguments property
234 |             // consider a escape of the arguments object.
235 |             if (!(NodeUtil.isGet(refParent)
236 |                 && refNode == ref.getParent().getFirstChild()
237 |                 && !isLValue(refParent))) {
238 |               return true;
239 |             }
240 |           }
241 |         }
242 |       }
243 |       return false;
244 |     }

Original range: 155-276
Documentation:

Method code:
155 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {
156 |     switch (n.getType()) {
157 |       // Childless expressions
158 |       case Token.FALSE:
159 |       case Token.NULL:
160 |       case Token.THIS:
161 |       case Token.TRUE:
162 |         validateChildless(n);
163 |         return;
164 | 
165 |       // General unary ops
166 |       case Token.DELPROP:
167 |       case Token.POS:
168 |       case Token.NEG:
169 |       case Token.NOT:
170 |       case Token.INC:
171 |       case Token.DEC:
172 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
173 |       case Token.VOID:
174 |       case Token.BITNOT:
175 |       case Token.CAST:
176 |         validateUnaryOp(n);
177 |         return;
178 | 
179 |       // General binary ops
180 |       case Token.COMMA:
181 |       case Token.OR:
182 |       case Token.AND:
183 |       case Token.BITOR:
184 |       case Token.BITXOR:
185 |       case Token.BITAND:
186 |       case Token.EQ:
187 |       case Token.NE:
188 |       case Token.SHEQ:
189 |       case Token.SHNE:
190 |       case Token.LT:
191 |       case Token.GT:
192 |       case Token.LE:
193 |       case Token.GE:
194 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
195 |       case Token.IN:
196 |       case Token.LSH:
197 |       case Token.RSH:
198 |       case Token.URSH:
199 |       case Token.SUB:
200 |       case Token.ADD:
201 |       case Token.MUL:
202 |       case Token.MOD:
203 |       case Token.DIV:
204 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
205 |         return;
206 | 
207 |       // Assignments
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
213 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
214 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
215 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
216 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
217 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
218 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
219 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
220 |         validateAssignmentExpression(n);
221 |         return;
222 | 
223 |       case Token.HOOK:
224 |         validateTrinaryOp(n);
225 |         return;
226 | 
227 |       // Node types that require special handling
228 |       case Token.STRING:
229 |         validateString(n);
230 |         return;
231 | 
232 |       case Token.NUMBER:
233 |         validateNumber(n);
234 |         return;
235 | 
236 |       case Token.NAME:
237 |         validateName(n);
238 |         return;
239 | 
240 |       case Token.GETELEM:
241 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
242 |         return;
243 | 
244 |       case Token.GETPROP:
245 |         validateGetProp(n);
246 |         return;
247 | 
248 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
249 |         validateArrayLit(n);
250 |         return;
251 | 
252 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
253 |         validateObjectLit(n);
254 |         return;
255 | 
256 |       case Token.REGEXP:
257 |         validateRegExpLit(n);
258 |         return;
259 | 
260 |       case Token.CALL:
261 |         validateCall(n);
262 |         return;
263 | 
264 |       case Token.NEW:
265 |         validateNew(n);
266 |         return;
267 | 
268 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
269 |         validateFunctionExpression(n);
270 |         return;
271 | 
272 |       default:
273 |         violation("Expected expression but was "
274 |             + Token.name(n.getType()), n);

===== M0497 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 946-1005
Documentation:

Method code:
946 |   private void toString(
947 |       StringBuilder sb,
948 |       boolean printSource,
949 |       boolean printAnnotations,
950 |       boolean printType) {
951 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
952 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
953 |       sb.append(' ');
954 |       sb.append(getString());
955 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
956 |       sb.append(' ');
957 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
958 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
959 |       // toStringTree.
960 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
961 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
962 |       } else {
963 |         sb.append(first.getString());
964 |       }
965 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
966 |       sb.append(' ');
967 |       sb.append(getDouble());
968 |     }
969 |     if (printSource) {
970 |       int lineno = getLineno();
971 |       if (lineno != -1) {
972 |         sb.append(' ');
973 |         sb.append(lineno);
974 |       }
975 |     }
976 | 
977 |     if (printAnnotations) {
978 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
979 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
980 |         int type = keys[i];
981 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
982 |         sb.append(" [");
983 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
984 |         sb.append(": ");
985 |         String value;
986 |         switch (type) {
987 |           default:
988 |             value = x.toString();
989 |             break;
990 |         }
991 |         sb.append(value);
992 |         sb.append(']');
993 |       }
994 |     }
995 | 
996 |     if (printType) {
997 |       if (jsType != null) {
998 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
999 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
1000 |           sb.append(" : ");
1001 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
1002 |         }
1003 |       }
1004 |     }
1005 |   }

===== M0506 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 316-363
Documentation:

Method code:
316 |   @Override
317 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
318 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
319 |       return true;
320 |     }
321 | 
322 |     // Union types
323 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
324 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
325 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
326 |       return false;
327 |     }
328 | 
329 |     // record types
330 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
331 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     // Interfaces
335 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
336 |     // to the interface instance.
337 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
338 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
339 | 
340 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
341 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
342 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
343 |           return true;
344 |         }
345 |       }
346 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
347 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
348 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
349 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
350 |           return true;
351 |         }
352 |       }
353 |     }
354 | 
355 |     // other prototype based objects
356 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
357 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
358 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
359 |       // to avoid guessing.
360 |       return true;
361 |     }
362 |     return thatObj != null && isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
363 |   }

/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1444 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1445 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false, false)) {
1446 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1447 |     }
1448 | 
1449 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1450 |     Node n, n2;
1451 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1452 |          res == null && n != null;
1453 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1454 |       if (node2 == null) {
1455 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1456 |       }
1457 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1458 |       if (res != null) {
1459 |         return res;
1460 |       }
1461 |     }
1462 |     return res;
1463 |   }

===== M0544 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeIterators.LocalVarMotion.advanceLookAhead(boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeIterators.java
Original range: 220-288
Documentation:

Method code:
220 |     private void advanceLookAhead(boolean atStart) {
221 |       if (!atStart) {
222 |         if (lookAhead == null) {
223 |           return;
224 |         }
225 | 
226 |         // Don't advance past a reference to the variable that we're trying
227 |         // to inline.
228 |         Node curNode = iterator.current();
229 |         if (curNode.isName() &&
230 |             varName.equals(curNode.getString())) {
231 |           lookAhead = null;
232 |           return;
233 |         }
234 |       }
235 | 
236 |       if (!iterator.hasNext()) {
237 |         lookAhead = null;
238 |         return;
239 |       }
240 | 
241 |       Node nextNode = iterator.next();
242 |       Node nextParent = iterator.currentParent();
243 |       int type = nextNode.getType();
244 | 
245 |       if (valueHasSideEffects) {
246 |         // Reject anything that might read state
247 |         boolean readsState = false;
248 | 
249 |         if (// Any read of a different variable.
250 |             (nextNode.isName() && !varName.equals(nextNode.getString())) ||
251 |             // Any read of a property.
252 |             (nextNode.isGetProp() || nextNode.isGetElem())) {
253 | 
254 |           // If this is a simple assign, we'll be ok.
255 |           if (nextParent == null ||
256 |               !NodeUtil.isVarOrSimpleAssignLhs(nextNode, nextParent)) {
257 |             readsState = true;
258 |           }
259 | 
260 |         } else if (nextNode.isCall() || nextNode.isNew()) {
261 |           // This isn't really an important case. In most cases when we use
262 |           // CALL or NEW, we're invoking it on a NAME or a GETPROP. And in the
263 |           // few cases where we're not, it's because we have an anonymous
264 |           // function that escapes the variable we're worried about. But we
265 |           // include this for completeness.
266 |           readsState = true;
267 |         }
268 | 
269 |         if (readsState) {
270 |           lookAhead = null;
271 |           return;
272 |         }
273 |       }
274 | 
275 |       // Reject anything that might modify relevant state. We assume that
276 |       // nobody relies on variables being undeclared, which will break
277 |       // constructions like:
278 |       //   var a = b;
279 |       //   var b = 3;
280 |       //   alert(a);
281 |       if (NodeUtil.nodeTypeMayHaveSideEffects(nextNode) && type != Token.NAME
282 |           || type == Token.NAME && nextParent.isCatch()) {
283 |         lookAhead = null;
284 |         return;
285 |       }
286 | 
287 |       lookAhead = nextNode;
288 |     }

112 |         return;

115 |         return;

===== M0551 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 399-424
Documentation:

Method code:
399 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
400 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
401 |     // two cases:
402 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
403 |     // and
404 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
405 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
406 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
407 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
408 |     //
409 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
410 |     //
411 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
412 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
413 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
414 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
415 |     //
416 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
417 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
418 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
419 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
420 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
421 |           registry, getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
422 |     }
423 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
424 |   }

===== M0554 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 552-572
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
552 |   @Override
553 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
554 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
555 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
556 |     if (unknown) {
557 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
558 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
559 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
560 |         unknown = false;
561 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
562 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
563 |             unknown = true;
564 |             break;
565 |           }
566 |         }
567 |       } else {
568 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
569 |       }
570 |     }
571 |     return unknown;
572 |   }

/**
     * Mark names and properties that are constants by convention.

Method code:
422 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
423 |       Preconditions.checkState(
424 |           n.isName()
425 |           || n.isString()
426 |           || n.isStringKey()
427 |           || n.isGetterDef()
428 |           || n.isSetterDef());
429 | 
430 |       // There are only two cases where a string token
431 |       // may be a variable reference: The right side of a GETPROP
432 |       // or an OBJECTLIT key.
433 |       boolean isObjLitKey = NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n);

435 |           (parent.isGetProp() &&
436 |            parent.getLastChild() == n);
437 |       if (n.isName() || isProperty) {
438 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
439 |         if (!isMarkedConstant &&
440 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(

442 |           if (assertOnChange) {

446 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
447 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
448 |           }
449 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
450 |         }
451 |       }

===== M0564 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1184-1204
Documentation:

Method code:
1184 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1185 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1186 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1187 |       return s;
1188 |     }
1189 | 
1190 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-Latin characters if needed.
1191 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1192 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1193 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1194 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1195 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1196 |       // crazy.
1197 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1198 |         sb.append(c);
1199 |       } else {
1200 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1201 |       }
1202 |     }
1203 |     return sb.toString();
1204 |   }

===== M0591 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 120-281
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
120 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
121 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
122 |     // just bail out early here.
123 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
124 |       return this;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
128 | 
129 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
130 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
131 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
132 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
133 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
134 |     }
135 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
136 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
137 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
138 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
139 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
140 |         }
141 |       } else {
142 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
143 |           return this;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
147 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
148 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
149 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
150 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
151 |           FunctionType other =
152 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
153 |           FunctionType supremum =
154 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
155 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
156 |           result = null;
157 |           return this;
158 |         }
159 | 
160 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
161 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
162 |         // one another.
163 |         int currentIndex = 0;
164 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
165 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
166 |           boolean removeCurrent = false;
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplateTypes() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplateTypes()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 | 
184 |             // Because "Foo" and "Foo.<?>" are roughly equivalent
185 |             // templatized types, special care is needed when building the
186 |             // union. For example:
187 |             //   Object is consider a subtype of Object.<string>
188 |             // but we want to leave "Object" not "Object.<string>" when
189 |             // building the subtype.
190 |             //
191 | 
192 |             if (alternate.isTemplatizedType() || current.isTemplatizedType()) {
193 |               // Cases:
194 |               // 1) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Object ==> Object
195 |               // 2) alternate:Array.<string> and current:Array ==> Array
196 |               // 3) alternate:Object.<string> and
197 |               //    current:Array ==> Array|Object.<string>
198 |               // 4) alternate:Object and current:Array.<string> ==> Object
199 |               // 5) alternate:Array and current:Array.<string> ==> Array
200 |               // 6) alternate:Array and
201 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Array|Object.<string>
202 |               // 7) alternate:Array.<string> and
203 |               //    current:Array.<number> ==> Array.<?>
204 |               // 8) alternate:Array.<string> and
205 |               //    current:Array.<string> ==> Array.<string>
206 |               // 9) alternate:Array.<string> and
207 |               //    current:Object.<string> ==> Object.<string>|Array.<string>
208 | 
209 |               if (!current.isTemplatizedType()) {
210 |                 if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
211 |                   // case 1, 2
212 |                   return this;
213 |                 }
214 |                 // case 3: leave current, add alternate
215 |               } else if (!alternate.isTemplatizedType()) {
216 |                 if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
217 |                   // case 4, 5
218 |                   removeCurrent = true;
219 |                 }
220 |                 // case 6: leave current, add alternate
221 |               } else {
222 |                 Preconditions.checkState(current.isTemplatizedType()
223 |                     && alternate.isTemplatizedType());
224 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedAlternate = alternate.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
225 |                 TemplatizedType templatizedCurrent = current.toMaybeTemplatizedType();
226 | 
227 |                 if (templatizedCurrent.wrapsSameRawType(templatizedAlternate)) {
228 |                   if (alternate.getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
229 |                       current.getTemplateTypeMap(),
230 |                       EquivalenceMethod.IDENTITY)) {
231 |                     // case 8
232 |                     return this;
233 |                   } else {
234 |                     // TODO(johnlenz): should we leave both types?
235 |                     // case 7: add a merged alternate
236 |                     // We currently merge to the templatized types to "unknown"
237 |                     // which is equivalent to the raw type.
238 |                     JSType merged = templatizedCurrent
239 |                         .getReferencedObjTypeInternal();
240 |                     return addAlternate(merged);
241 |                   }
242 |                 }
243 |                 // case 9: leave current, add alternate
244 |               }
245 |               // Otherwise leave both templatized types.
246 |             } else if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
247 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
248 |               return this;
249 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
250 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
251 |               removeCurrent = true;
252 |             }
253 |           }
254 | 
255 |           if (removeCurrent) {
256 |             it.remove();
257 | 
258 |             if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
259 |               functionTypePosition = -1;
260 |             } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
261 |               functionTypePosition--;
262 |               currentIndex--;
263 |             }
264 |           }
265 |           currentIndex++;
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
269 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
270 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
271 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         alternates.add(alternate);
275 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
276 |       }
277 |     } else {
278 |       result = null;
279 |     }
280 |     return this;
281 |   }

===== M0592 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.isVarInlineForbidden(Var)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 346-357
Documentation:
/**
     * Whether the given variable is forbidden from being inlined.
     */
Method code:
346 |     private boolean isVarInlineForbidden(Var var) {
347 |       // A variable may not be inlined if:
348 |       // 1) The variable is exported,
349 |       // 2) A reference to the variable has been inlined. We're downstream
350 |       //    of the mechanism that creates variable references, so we don't
351 |       //    have a good way to update the reference. Just punt on it.
352 |       // 3) Don't inline the special RENAME_PROPERTY_FUNCTION_NAME
353 |       return var.isExtern()
354 |           || compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(var.name)
355 |           || RenameProperties.RENAME_PROPERTY_FUNCTION_NAME.equals(var.name)
356 |           || staleVars.contains(var);
357 |     }

===== M0600 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2479-2488
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2479 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2480 |     int len = s.length();
2481 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2482 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2483 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2484 |         return false;
2485 |       }
2486 |     }
2487 |     return true;
2488 |   }

===== M0601 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 107-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
107 |   public static ParseResult parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
108 |                                   String sourceString,
109 |                                   Config config,
110 |                                   ErrorReporter errorReporter,
111 |                                   Logger logger) throws IOException {
112 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
113 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
114 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
115 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
116 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
117 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
118 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
119 | 
120 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
121 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
122 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
123 | 
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return new ParseResult(root, astRoot);
147 |   }

===== M0602 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 107-771
Documentation:

Method code:
107 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
108 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
109 |       return;
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     int type = n.getType();
113 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
114 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
115 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
116 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
117 | 
118 |     // Handle all binary operators
119 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
120 |       Preconditions.checkState(
121 |           childCount == 2,
122 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
123 |           opstr, childCount);
124 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
125 | 
126 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
127 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
128 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
129 | 
130 |       // Handle associativity.
131 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
132 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
133 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
134 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
135 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
136 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
137 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
138 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
139 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
140 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
141 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
142 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
143 |       } else {
144 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
145 |       }
146 |       return;
147 |     }
148 | 
149 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
150 | 
151 |     switch (type) {
152 |       case Token.TRY: {
153 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
154 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
155 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
156 | 
157 |         add("try");
158 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
159 | 
160 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
161 |         // isn't a catch block
162 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
163 |         if (catchblock != null) {
164 |           add(catchblock);
165 |         }
166 | 
167 |         if (childCount == 3) {
168 |           add("finally");
169 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
170 |         }
171 |         break;
172 |       }
173 | 
174 |       case Token.CATCH:
175 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
176 |         add("catch(");
177 |         add(first);
178 |         add(")");
179 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
180 |         break;
181 | 
182 |       case Token.THROW:
183 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
184 |         add("throw");
185 |         add(first);
186 | 
187 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
188 |         // parse this.
189 |         cc.endStatement(true);
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.RETURN:
193 |         add("return");
194 |         if (childCount == 1) {
195 |           add(first);
196 |         } else {
197 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
198 |         }
199 |         cc.endStatement();
200 |         break;
201 | 
202 |       case Token.VAR:
203 |         if (first != null) {
204 |           add("var ");
205 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
206 |         }
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
210 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
211 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.NAME:
215 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
216 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
217 |         } else {
218 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
219 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
220 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
221 |           if (first.isComma()) {
222 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
223 |           } else {
224 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
225 |             // precedence.
226 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
227 |           }
228 |         }
229 |         break;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
232 |         add("[");
233 |         addArrayList(first);
234 |         add("]");
235 |         break;
236 | 
237 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
238 |         add("(");
239 |         addList(first);
240 |         add(")");
241 |         break;
242 | 
243 |       case Token.COMMA:
244 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
245 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context,
246 |             getContextForNoInOperator(context), 0, 0);
247 |         break;
248 | 
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
251 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
252 |         break;
253 | 
254 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
255 |       case Token.VOID:
256 |       case Token.NOT:
257 |       case Token.BITNOT:
258 |       case Token.POS: {
259 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
260 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
261 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
262 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
263 |         break;
264 |       }
265 | 
266 |       case Token.NEG: {
267 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
268 | 
269 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
270 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
271 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
272 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
273 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
274 |         } else {
275 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
276 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
277 |         }
278 | 
279 |         break;
280 |       }
281 | 
282 |       case Token.HOOK: {
283 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
284 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
285 |         Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
286 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
287 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
288 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, rhsContext);
289 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
290 |         addExpr(last, 1, rhsContext);
291 |         break;
292 |       }
293 | 
294 |       case Token.REGEXP:
295 |         if (!first.isString() ||
296 |             !last.isString()) {
297 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
298 |         }
299 | 
300 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
301 | 
302 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
303 |         if (childCount == 2) {
304 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
305 |         } else {
306 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
307 |           add(regexp);
308 |         }
309 |         break;
310 | 
311 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
312 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
313 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
314 |         }
315 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
316 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
317 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
318 |           add("(");
319 |         }
320 | 
321 |         add("function");
322 |         add(first);
323 | 
324 |         add(first.getNext());
325 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
326 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
327 | 
328 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
329 |           add(")");
330 |         }
331 |         break;
332 | 
333 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
334 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
335 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
336 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
337 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
338 | 
339 |         // Get methods are unnamed
340 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
341 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
342 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
343 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
344 |           add("get ");
345 |         } else {
346 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
347 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
348 |           add("set ");
349 |         }
350 | 
351 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
352 |         String name = n.getString();
353 |         Node fn = first;
354 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
355 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
356 | 
357 |         // Add the property name.
358 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
359 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
360 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
361 |             // Unicode escaped.
362 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
363 |           add(name);
364 |         } else {
365 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
366 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
367 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
368 |             cc.addNumber(d);
369 |           } else {
370 |             addJsString(n);
371 |           }
372 |         }
373 | 
374 |         add(parameters);
375 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
376 |         break;
377 | 
378 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
379 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
380 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
381 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
382 |         }
383 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
384 |         if (preserveBlock) {
385 |           cc.beginBlock();
386 |         }
387 | 
388 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
389 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
390 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
391 |                 !preserveBlock &&
392 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
393 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
394 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
395 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
396 | 
397 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
398 |           if (c.isVar()) {
399 |             cc.endStatement();
400 |           }
401 | 
402 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
403 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
404 |           }
405 | 
406 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
407 |           // because top-level statements are more homogeneous.
408 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
409 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
410 |           }
411 |         }
412 |         if (preserveBlock) {
413 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
414 |         }
415 |         break;
416 |       }
417 | 
418 |       case Token.FOR:
419 |         if (childCount == 4) {
420 |           add("for(");
421 |           if (first.isVar()) {
422 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
423 |           } else {
424 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
425 |           }
426 |           add(";");
427 |           add(first.getNext());
428 |           add(";");
429 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
430 |           add(")");
431 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
432 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
433 |         } else {
434 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
435 |           add("for(");
436 |           add(first);
437 |           add("in");
438 |           add(first.getNext());
439 |           add(")");
440 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
441 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
442 |         }
443 |         break;
444 | 
445 |       case Token.DO:
446 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
447 |         add("do");
448 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
449 |         add("while(");
450 |         add(last);
451 |         add(")");
452 |         cc.endStatement();
453 |         break;
454 | 
455 |       case Token.WHILE:
456 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
457 |         add("while(");
458 |         add(first);
459 |         add(")");
460 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
461 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
462 |         break;
463 | 
464 |       case Token.EMPTY:
465 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
466 |         break;
467 | 
468 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
469 |         Preconditions.checkState(
470 |             childCount == 2,
471 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
472 |         Preconditions.checkState(
473 |             last.isString(),
474 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
475 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
476 |         if (needsParens) {
477 |           add("(");
478 |         }
479 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
480 |         if (needsParens) {
481 |           add(")");
482 |         }
483 |         if (this.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3
484 |             && TokenStream.isKeyword(last.getString())) {
485 |           // Check for ECMASCRIPT3 keywords.
486 |           add("[");
487 |           add(last);
488 |           add("]");
489 |         } else {
490 |           add(".");
491 |           addIdentifier(last.getString());
492 |         }
493 |         break;
494 |       }
495 | 
496 |       case Token.GETELEM:
497 |         Preconditions.checkState(
498 |             childCount == 2,
499 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
500 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
501 |         add("[");
502 |         add(first.getNext());
503 |         add("]");
504 |         break;
505 | 
506 |       case Token.WITH:
507 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
508 |         add("with(");
509 |         add(first);
510 |         add(")");
511 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
512 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
513 |         break;
514 | 
515 |       case Token.INC:
516 |       case Token.DEC: {
517 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
518 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
519 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
520 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
521 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
522 |         if (postProp != 0) {
523 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
524 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
525 |         } else {
526 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
527 |           add(first);
528 |         }
529 |         break;
530 |       }
531 | 
532 |       case Token.CALL:
533 |         // We have two special cases here:
534 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
535 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
536 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
537 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
538 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
539 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
540 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
541 |         // that must be preserved.
542 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
543 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
544 |           add("(0,");
545 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
546 |           add(")");
547 |         } else {
548 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
549 |         }
550 |         add("(");
551 |         addList(first.getNext());
552 |         add(")");
553 |         break;
554 | 
555 |       case Token.IF:
556 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
557 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
558 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
559 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
560 |           cc.beginBlock();
561 |         }
562 | 
563 |         add("if(");
564 |         add(first);
565 |         add(")");
566 | 
567 |         if (hasElse) {
568 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
569 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
570 |           add("else");
571 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
572 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
573 |         } else {
574 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
575 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
576 |         }
577 | 
578 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
579 |           cc.endBlock();
580 |         }
581 |         break;
582 | 
583 |       case Token.NULL:
584 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
585 |         cc.addConstant("null");
586 |         break;
587 | 
588 |       case Token.THIS:
589 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
590 |         add("this");
591 |         break;
592 | 
593 |       case Token.FALSE:
594 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
595 |         cc.addConstant("false");
596 |         break;
597 | 
598 |       case Token.TRUE:
599 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
600 |         cc.addConstant("true");
601 |         break;
602 | 
603 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
604 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
605 |         add("continue");
606 |         if (childCount == 1) {
607 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
608 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
609 |           }
610 |           add(" ");
611 |           add(first);
612 |         }
613 |         cc.endStatement();
614 |         break;
615 | 
616 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
617 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
618 |         add("debugger");
619 |         cc.endStatement();
620 |         break;
621 | 
622 |       case Token.BREAK:
623 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
624 |         add("break");
625 |         if (childCount == 1) {
626 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
627 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
628 |           }
629 |           add(" ");
630 |           add(first);
631 |         }
632 |         cc.endStatement();
633 |         break;
634 | 
635 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
636 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
637 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
638 |         cc.endStatement();
639 |         break;
640 | 
641 |       case Token.NEW:
642 |         add("new ");
643 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
644 | 
645 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
646 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
647 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
648 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
649 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
650 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
651 |         }
652 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
653 | 
654 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
655 |         Node next = first.getNext();
656 |         if (next != null) {
657 |           add("(");
658 |           addList(next);
659 |           add(")");
660 |         }
661 |         break;
662 | 
663 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
664 |         Preconditions.checkState(
665 |             childCount == 1, "Object lit key must have 1 child");
666 |         addJsString(n);
667 |         break;
668 | 
669 |       case Token.STRING:
670 |         Preconditions.checkState(
671 |             childCount == 0, "A string may not have children");
672 |         addJsString(n);
673 |         break;
674 | 
675 |       case Token.DELPROP:
676 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
677 |         add("delete ");
678 |         add(first);
679 |         break;
680 | 
681 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
682 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
683 |         if (needsParens) {
684 |           add("(");
685 |         }
686 |         add("{");
687 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
688 |           if (c != first) {
689 |             cc.listSeparator();
690 |           }
691 | 
692 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
693 |             add(c);
694 |           } else {
695 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isStringKey());
696 |             String key = c.getString();
697 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
698 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
699 |             if (!c.isQuotedString()
700 |                 && !(languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3
701 |                     && TokenStream.isKeyword(key))
702 |                 && TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key)
703 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
704 |                 // were Unicode escaped.
705 |                 && NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
706 |               add(key);
707 |             } else {
708 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
709 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
710 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
711 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
712 |               } else {
713 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
714 |               }
715 |             }
716 |             add(":");
717 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
718 |           }
719 |         }
720 |         add("}");
721 |         if (needsParens) {
722 |           add(")");
723 |         }
724 |         break;
725 |       }
726 | 
727 |       case Token.SWITCH:
728 |         add("switch(");
729 |         add(first);
730 |         add(")");
731 |         cc.beginBlock();
732 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
733 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
734 |         break;
735 | 
736 |       case Token.CASE:
737 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
738 |         add("case ");
739 |         add(first);
740 |         addCaseBody(last);
741 |         break;
742 | 
743 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
744 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
745 |         add("default");
746 |         addCaseBody(first);
747 |         break;
748 | 
749 |       case Token.LABEL:
750 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
751 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
752 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
753 |         }
754 |         add(first);
755 |         add(":");
756 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
757 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
758 |         break;
759 | 
760 |       case Token.CAST:
761 |         add("(");
762 |         add(first);
763 |         add(")");
764 |         break;
765 | 
766 |       default:
767 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
768 |     }
769 | 
770 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
771 |   }

===== M0603 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 239-270
Documentation:

Method code:
239 |     @Override
240 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
241 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
242 |       if (n.isName()) {
243 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
244 |           return;
245 |         }
246 | 
247 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
248 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top-level variable.
249 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
250 |         if (var != null) {
251 |           info = var.getJSDocInfo();
252 |         }
253 | 
254 |         boolean shouldBeConstant =
255 |             (info != null && info.isConstant()) ||
256 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
257 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent);
258 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
259 |         if (shouldBeConstant && !isMarkedConstant) {
260 |           if (assertOnChange) {
261 |             String name = n.getString();
262 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
263 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
264 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
265 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
266 |           }
267 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
268 |         }
269 |       }
270 |     }

/**
   * Detaches child from Node and replaces it with newChild.

Method code:
713 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {
714 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
715 |         "The new child node has siblings.");
716 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.parent == null,
717 |         "The new child node already has a parent.");
718 | 
719 |     // Copy over important information.
720 |     newChild.copyInformationFrom(child);
721 | 
722 |     newChild.next = child.next;
723 |     newChild.parent = this;
724 |     if (child == first) {
725 |       first = newChild;
726 |     } else {
727 |       Node prev = getChildBefore(child);
728 |       prev.next = newChild;
729 |     }
730 |     if (child == last) {
731 |       last = newChild;
732 |     }
733 |     child.next = null;
734 |     child.parent = null;
735 |   }
