===== M0623 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType,EquivalenceMethod)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 571-639
Documentation:
/**
   * An equivalence visitor.
   */
Method code:
571 |   boolean checkEquivalenceHelper(JSType that, EquivalenceMethod eqMethod) {
572 |     if (this == that) {
573 |       return true;
574 |     }
575 | 
576 |     boolean thisUnknown = isUnknownType();
577 |     boolean thatUnknown = that.isUnknownType();
578 |     if (thisUnknown || thatUnknown) {
579 |       if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.INVARIANT) {
580 |         // If we're checking for invariance, the unknown type is invariant
581 |         // with everyone.
582 |         return true;
583 |       } else if (eqMethod == EquivalenceMethod.DATA_FLOW) {
584 |         // If we're checking data flow, then two types are the same if they're
585 |         // both unknown.
586 |         return thisUnknown && thatUnknown;
587 |       } else if (thisUnknown && thatUnknown &&
588 |           (isNominalType() ^ that.isNominalType())) {
589 |         // If they're both unknown, but one is a nominal type and the other
590 |         // is not, then we should fail out immediately. This ensures that
591 |         // we won't unbox the unknowns further down.
592 |         return false;
593 |       }
594 |     }
595 | 
596 |     if (isUnionType() && that.isUnionType()) {
597 |       return toMaybeUnionType().checkUnionEquivalenceHelper(
598 |           that.toMaybeUnionType(), eqMethod);
599 |     }
600 | 
601 |     if (isFunctionType() && that.isFunctionType()) {
602 |       return toMaybeFunctionType().checkFunctionEquivalenceHelper(
603 |           that.toMaybeFunctionType(), eqMethod);
604 |     }
605 | 
606 |     if (isRecordType() && that.isRecordType()) {
607 |       return toMaybeRecordType().checkRecordEquivalenceHelper(
608 |           that.toMaybeRecordType(), eqMethod);
609 |     }
610 | 
611 |     if (!getTemplateTypeMap().checkEquivalenceHelper(
612 |         that.getTemplateTypeMap(), eqMethod)) {
613 |       return false;
614 |     }
615 | 
616 |     if (isNominalType() && that.isNominalType()) {
617 |       return toObjectType().getReferenceName().equals(
618 |           that.toObjectType().getReferenceName());
619 |     }
620 | 
621 |     // Unbox other proxies.
622 |     if (this instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
623 |       return ((ProxyObjectType) this)
624 |           .getReferencedTypeInternal().checkEquivalenceHelper(
625 |               that, eqMethod);
626 |     }
627 | 
628 |     if (that instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
629 |       return checkEquivalenceHelper(
630 |           ((ProxyObjectType) that).getReferencedTypeInternal(),
631 |           eqMethod);
632 |     }
633 | 
634 |     // Relies on the fact that for the base {@link JSType}, only one
635 |     // instance of each sub-type will ever be created in a given registry, so
636 |     // there is no need to verify members. If the object pointers are not
637 |     // identical, then the type member must be different.
638 |     return this == that;
639 |   }

===== M0629 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 609-624
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
609 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
610 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
611 |       Set<String> directives = null;
612 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
613 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
614 |         if (directives == null) {
615 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
616 |         } else {
617 |           directives.add(directive);
618 |         }
619 |       }
620 | 
621 |       if (directives != null) {
622 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
623 |       }
624 |     }

===== M0637 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 672-684
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
672 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
673 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
674 |     if (typeSet == null) {
675 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
676 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
677 |     }
678 | 
679 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
680 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
681 | 
682 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
683 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
684 |   }

     * Replace the given reference with the given value node.
     *

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.collectAliasCandidates(NodeTraversal,ReferenceMap)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 167-188
Documentation:
/**
     * If any of the variables are well-defined and alias other variables,
     * mark them as aliasing candidates.
     */
Method code:
167 |     private void collectAliasCandidates(NodeTraversal t,
168 |         ReferenceMap referenceMap) {
169 |       if (mode != Mode.CONSTANTS_ONLY) {
170 |         for (Iterator<Var> it = t.getScope().getVars(); it.hasNext();) {
171 |           Var v = it.next();
172 |           ReferenceCollection referenceInfo = referenceMap.getReferences(v);
173 | 
174 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): Don't handle variables that are never used.
175 |           // The tests are much easier to write if you don't, and there's
176 |           // another pass that handles unused variables much more elegantly.
177 |           if (referenceInfo != null && referenceInfo.references.size() >= 2 &&
178 |               referenceInfo.isWellDefined() &&
179 |               referenceInfo.isAssignedOnceInLifetime()) {
180 |             Reference init = referenceInfo.getInitializingReference();
181 |             Node value = init.getAssignedValue();
182 |             if (value != null && value.isName()) {
183 |               aliasCandidates.put(value, new AliasCandidate(v, referenceInfo));
184 |             }
185 |           }
186 |         }
187 |       }
188 |     }

===== M0673 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1229-1231
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1229 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1230 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1231 |   }

===== M0684 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ReferenceCollectingCallback.BasicBlock.provablyExecutesBefore(BasicBlock)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ReferenceCollectingCallback.java
Original range: 726-745
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether this block is guaranteed to begin executing before
     * the given block does.
     */
Method code:
726 |     boolean provablyExecutesBefore(BasicBlock thatBlock) {
727 |       // If thatBlock is a descendant of this block, and there are no hoisted
728 |       // blocks between them, then this block must start before thatBlock.
729 |       BasicBlock currentBlock;
730 |       for (currentBlock = thatBlock;
731 |            currentBlock != null && currentBlock != this;
732 |            currentBlock = currentBlock.getParent()) {
733 |         if (currentBlock.isHoisted) {
734 |           return false;
735 |         }
736 |       }
737 | 
738 |       if (currentBlock == this) {
739 |         return true;
740 |       }
741 |       if (isGlobalScopeBlock() && thatBlock.isGlobalScopeBlock()) {
742 |         return true;
743 |       }
744 |       return false;
745 |     }

===== M0690 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3084-3112
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3084 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3085 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3086 |     if (info == null) {
3087 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3088 |       if (parent == null) {
3089 |         return null;
3090 |       }
3091 | 
3092 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3093 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3094 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3095 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3096 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent)) {
3097 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3098 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3099 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3100 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3101 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3102 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3103 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3104 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3105 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3106 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3107 |       } else if (parent.isCast()) {
3108 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3109 |       }
3110 |     }
3111 |     return info;
3112 |   }

===== M0692 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.add(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 180-203
Documentation:

Method code:
180 |   void add(String newcode) {
181 |     maybeEndStatement();
182 | 
183 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
184 |       return;
185 |     }
186 | 
187 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
188 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
189 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
190 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
191 |       // For example: "return foo;"
192 |       append(" ");
193 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
194 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
195 |       // For example,
196 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
197 |       // is valid and should print like
198 |       // / // / /
199 |       append(" ");
200 |     }
201 | 
202 |     append(newcode);
203 |   }

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
796 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
797 |     if (item == null) {
798 |       return null;
799 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
800 |       return item.getNext();
801 |     } else {
802 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
803 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
804 |         return item.chain(result);
805 |       } else {
806 |         return item;
807 |       }
808 |     }
809 |   }

465 |     void maybeLineBreak() {

468 |           startNewLine();
469 |           sawFunction = false;
470 |         }
471 |       }
472 |

===== M0733 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 83-118
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |   private void scanRoot(Node n) {
84 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
85 |       if (inputId == null) {
86 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
87 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
88 |         // from the AST.
89 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
90 |       }
91 | 
92 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
93 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
94 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
95 | 
96 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
97 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
98 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
99 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
100 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
101 |       }
102 | 
103 |       // Args: Declare function variables
104 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
105 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
106 |            a = a.getNext()) {
107 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
108 |         declareVar(a);
109 |       }
110 | 
111 |       // Body
112 |       scanVars(body);
113 |     } else {
114 |       // It's the global block
115 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
116 |       scanVars(n);
117 |     }
118 |   }

===== M0739 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 123-177
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
123 |   private void scanVars(Node n) {
124 |     switch (n.getType()) {
125 |       case Token.VAR:
126 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
127 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
128 |              child != null;) {
129 |           Node next = child.getNext();
130 |           declareVar(child);
131 |           child = next;
132 |         }
133 |         return;
134 | 
135 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
136 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
137 |           return;
138 |         }
139 | 
140 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
141 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
142 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
143 |           return;
144 |         }
145 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
146 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
147 | 
148 |       case Token.CATCH:
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
151 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
152 |         // is the code block
153 | 
154 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
155 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
156 | 
157 |         declareVar(var);
158 |         scanVars(block);
159 |         return;  // only one child to scan
160 | 
161 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
162 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
163 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
164 |         break;
165 |     }
166 | 
167 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
168 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
169 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
170 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
171 |            child != null;) {
172 |         Node next = child.getNext();
173 |         scanVars(child);
174 |         child = next;
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 |   }

/** Checks that the scope roots marked as changed have indeed changed */
Method code:
3343 |   public static void verifyScopeChanges(Map<Node, Node> map,
3344 |       Node main, boolean verifyUnchangedNodes,
3345 |       AbstractCompiler compiler) {
3346 |     // compiler is passed only to call compiler.toSource during debugging to see
3347 |     // mismatches in scopes
3348 | 
3349 |     // If verifyUnchangedNodes is false, we are comparing the initial AST to the
3350 |     // final AST. Don't check unmarked nodes b/c they may have been changed by
3351 |     // non-loopable passes.
3352 |     // If verifyUnchangedNodes is true, we are comparing the ASTs before & after
3353 |     // a pass. Check all scope roots.
3354 |     final Map<Node, Node> mtoc = map;
3355 |     final boolean checkUnchanged = verifyUnchangedNodes;
3356 |     Node clone = mtoc.get(main);
3357 |     if (main.getChangeTime() > clone.getChangeTime()) {
3358 |       Preconditions.checkState(!main.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3359 |     } else if (checkUnchanged) {
3360 |       Preconditions.checkState(main.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3361 |     }
3362 |     visitPreOrder(main,
3363 |         new Visitor() {
3364 |           @Override
3365 |           public void visit(Node n) {
3366 |             if (n.isFunction() && mtoc.containsKey(n)) {
3367 |               Node clone = mtoc.get(n);
3368 |               if (n.getChangeTime() > clone.getChangeTime()) {
3369 |                 Preconditions.checkState(!n.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3370 |               } else if (checkUnchanged) {
3371 |                 Preconditions.checkState(n.isEquivalentToShallow(clone));
3372 |               }
3373 |             }
3374 |           }
3375 |         },
3376 |         Predicates.<Node>alwaysTrue());
3377 |   }

===== M0743 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineVariables.InliningBehavior.canMoveModerately(Reference,Reference)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineVariables.java
Original range: 598-630
Documentation:
/**
     * If the value of a variable is not constant, then it may read or modify
     * state. Therefore it cannot be moved past anything else that may modify
     * the value being read or read values that are modified.
     */
Method code:
598 |     private boolean canMoveModerately(
599 |         Reference initialization,
600 |         Reference reference) {
601 |       // Check if declaration can be inlined without passing
602 |       // any side-effect causing nodes.
603 |       Iterator<Node> it;
604 |       if (initialization.getParent().isVar()) {
605 |         it = NodeIterators.LocalVarMotion.forVar(
606 |             initialization.getNode(),     // NAME
607 |             initialization.getParent(),       // VAR
608 |             initialization.getGrandparent()); // VAR container
609 |       } else if (initialization.getParent().isAssign()) {
610 |         Preconditions.checkState(
611 |             initialization.getGrandparent().isExprResult());
612 |         it = NodeIterators.LocalVarMotion.forAssign(
613 |             initialization.getNode(),     // NAME
614 |             initialization.getParent(),       // ASSIGN
615 |             initialization.getGrandparent(),  // EXPR_RESULT
616 |             initialization.getGrandparent().getParent()); // EXPR container
617 |       } else {
618 |         throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected initialization parent " +
619 |             initialization.getParent().toStringTree());
620 |       }
621 |       Node targetName = reference.getNode();
622 |       while (it.hasNext()) {
623 |         Node curNode = it.next();
624 |         if (curNode == targetName) {
625 |           return true;
626 |         }
627 |       }
628 | 
629 |       return false;
630 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 515-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
515 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
516 |     int type = n.getType();
517 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
518 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
519 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
520 |     }
521 | 
522 |     curNode = n;
523 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) {
524 |       return;
525 |     }
526 | 
527 |     if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
528 |       traverseFunction(n, parent);
529 |     } else {
530 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
531 |         // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
532 |         // would no longer point to the true next
533 |         Node next = child.getNext();
534 |         traverseBranch(child, n);
535 |         child = next;
536 |       }
537 |     }
538 | 
539 |     curNode = n;
540 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
541 |   }

===== M0773 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 647-679
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
647 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
648 |       Preconditions.checkState(
649 |           functionBody.getParent().isFunction());
650 |       Node previous = null;
651 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
652 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
653 |       // are already in the right place.
654 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
655 |         previous = current;
656 |         current = current.getNext();
657 |       }
658 | 
659 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
660 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
661 |       while (current != null) {
662 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
663 |         Node next = current.getNext();
664 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
665 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
666 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
667 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
668 | 
669 |           // Read the function at the top of the function body (after any
670 |           // previous declarations).
671 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
672 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
673 |         } else {
674 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
675 |           previous = current;
676 |         }
677 |         current = next;
678 |       }
679 |     }

===== M0785 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 490-507
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
490 |     @Override
491 |     void maybeCutLine() {
492 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
493 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
494 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
495 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
496 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
497 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
498 |           prevCutPosition = position;
499 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition, true);
500 |           lineIndex++;
501 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
502 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
503 |         } else {
504 |           startNewLine();
505 |         }
506 |       }
507 |     }

===== M0789 =====

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 808-820
Documentation:

Method code:
808 |   private void validateMaximumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
809 |     boolean valid = false;
810 |     if (i == 1) {
811 |       valid = !n.hasMoreThanOneChild();
812 |     } else {
813 |       valid = n.getChildCount() <= i;
814 |     }
815 |     if (!valid) {
816 |       violation(
817 |           "Expected no more than " + i + " children, but was "
818 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
819 |     }
820 |   }
